Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the oceanography space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 918 and the convergence rate is O(455/n^4).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on oceanography structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 918. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 455. QED.
